# OSM Table Tennis Location Counts

A clean, country-by-country view of the staged OpenStreetMap table-tennis venue import. Countries are sorted by total locations, and cities inside each country are sorted from largest to smallest.

> Source: `supabase/seeds/osm/*.sql`, generated from OpenStreetMap via Overture Maps and GeoNames.

## At a Glance

| Metric | Count |
|---|---:|
| Total locations | 44,914 |
| Countries | 44 |
| Cities | 10,291 |
